Game: POWERBALL
Mode: WF - Walk-forward ("honest test: trained on earlier draws only")

====================================================================================================
GLOSSARY
====================================================================================================
WF - Walk-forward ("honest test: trained on earlier draws only")
  For every past draw that is scored, the method was trained only on the draws before it, then its prediction for that draw was compared with the actual result. This is exactly the position in real life, where the next draw is unknown when the prediction is made. It is an honest test of prediction.
IS - In-sample ("hindsight fit: trained on all draws, including the ones scored")
  The method was trained once on every draw in the history, including the draws it is then scored against, and its predictions were checked against those same draws. It shows how well the method describes results it has already seen. It is not a test of prediction, and its success figures can be inflated by hindsight. How much depends on the method. In a separate fair-draw illustration using this project's E_1 method, in-sample fitting alone produced an apparent gain of about 3 sigma; that is not a general value for all methods (R3 section 17.18.3).

MR0 - Division win profile
  One line: who has won the best prizes, best division first.
  Short explanation: counts how many times the track's entries would have won each prize division. Tracks are ranked by top-division wins first, then the next division, and so on. Top-division wins are extremely rare under a fair draw, so most tracks share the same counts at the top; the lower divisions carry most of the information.
MR1 - Surprisal-weighted division score; MR1-T - owner's top-division score
  One line: the average "rarity" of prizes won per draw, in bits.
  Short explanation: each division win is worth the bits of surprise it represents under a fair draw: about 6.0 bits for Powerball Division 9, up to 27.0 bits for Division 1. MR1 is the average bits won per draw, next to the fair-draw expectation. MR1-T keeps the owner's original weighting of the three top divisions (Division 1 90%, Division 2 7%, Division 3 3%); it is a declared preference score, not a statistical weight.
MR4 - Division sigma (excess wins)
  One line: how many sigma above fair-draw chance this track's division wins are.
  Short explanation: counts the track's wins in a set of divisions and asks how likely at least that many would be under a fair draw over the same draws. That probability is shown on the sigma scale used in science reporting. Zero wins is 0 excess sigma. The local figure treats the track alone; the global figures allow for how many tests were made.
MR5 - Rank position (existing R3 measure)
  One line: where the method's ranking placed the numbers that were actually drawn.
  Short explanation: R3's ratified evaluator measure: the average mid-rank of the 7 drawn main numbers in the method's full frozen ranking (1 = most favoured), and the drawn Powerball's mid-rank. Fair-draw expectations are 18.0 (Powerball main), 10.5 (Powerball) and 24.0 (Oz Lotto). Lower means the method tended to favour numbers that came up. It belongs to the method and variant, because TOP and WORST share one ranking.
MR7 - Total main-match excess (ChatGPT; core stage)
  One line: did the track hit more main numbers in total than fair draws would produce?
  Short explanation: counts every main-number match, prize or not. It is the most direct test of a steady advantage and applies to every method that produces tickets.
MR16 - Hit-rate estimate with exact interval
  One line: how often this track achieved a given kind of success, how uncertain that rate is, and what observed rate would be needed to reach 3 sigma.
  Short explanation: a success here is a yes/no event per draw, such as any prize division, a particular division, at least 2, 3 or 4 main matches, or a Powerball hit. MR16 shows the track's observed rate with an exact 95% interval and the fair-draw rate beside it. It also shows the observed rate needed to reach 3 sigma locally over this window, and the chance of reaching it if the method were truly 1.5x or 2x better. With a few hundred draws the intervals are wide, and seeing that plainly prevents striking-looking counts being over-read. It vetoes nothing.

Calibration labels:
  EXACT_LOCAL: Exact reference tail for this single track and sub-measure (for example binomial division counts, MR7, MR8, MR11).
  APPROX_UNCALIBRATED: Normal or other approximation not yet checked against N1. Shown, labelled.
  N1_CALIBRATED: Probability from N1, only for single-track statistics in the hit-sequence class (17.5A), where the replay is exact even for adaptive methods. The Monte Carlo uncertainty is shown.
  N1_FIXED_FORECAST_REFERENCE: N1 replay figure for any other statistic (cross-track maxima; measures scored with forecast weights or probabilities). Shown as a reference; not a calibrated probability for adaptive WF methods.
  SELECTION_UNVERIFIED: The table's opportunity set is not a fixed grid (17.3.7A), so exact or N1 tails are not calibrated. The figures are shown.
  BONFERRONI_REFERENCE_UNCALIBRATED: Bonferroni arithmetic applied where its prerequisites fail (an input not `EXACT_LOCAL`/`N1_CALIBRATED`, or a post-hoc family). Shown; not a guaranteed figure.
  IS_NOT_CALIBRATED: Any significance figure in an IS table. The number is shown, but because the method was fitted on these outcomes it is not a calibrated probability (R2 section 8.4).
  NOT_A_P_VALUE: Descriptive scores and Bayes factors.
  MR_EXACT_BOUND_EXCEEDED, P_BELOW_FLOAT64_RANGE, SIGMA_ABOVE_BOUND, N1_NOT_RUN: design v0.1r1 6.6 and 7.6.
Report statuses:
  MR_NOT_APPLICABLE: capability missing
  MR_INSUFFICIENT_DATA: below a measure's stated minimum
  MR_UNDEFINED: mathematically undefined, for example a period with fewer than 2 wins
  MR_NUMERIC_INVALID: non-finite or invalid input
  NULL_VARIANCE_ZERO: the reference variance is 0, so no sigma is defined; shown as neutral
  GRID_COMPLETE / GRID_INCOMPLETE / GRID_EMPTY: design v0.1r1 6.2A (track statuses).
Evidence tiers:
  DEVELOPMENT_WF_EXPLORATORY: walk-forward on development data that methods, windows and measures may have been designed against
  HOLDOUT_MODE_H: FINAL_HOLDOUT_10 after Mode H scoring
  PROSPECTIVE: forecasts frozen and published before their draws
  IS_DESCRIPTIVE: in-sample.
Pattern origins:
  PO-1 An unavoidable consequence of how the method works: Partly diagnosable: N1 fixed-forecast replays show some method-built patterns, but not all for adaptive methods (17.5A). Planted-effect and null synthetic tests (MRT-07). A full recursive simulator (MRO-10) would cover more.
  PO-2 An effect of fitting to past outcomes: Present in IS by construction; absent in WF by N-1 isolation. Shown by the IS-versus-WF comparison.
  PO-3 Chance, many comparisons or selection: Bonferroni, the N1 maximum statistic, the search log, predeclared cohorts.
  PO-4 A pattern that persists on genuinely new outcomes: Only HOLDOUT_MODE_H and PROSPECTIVE tiers can show it.
Methods:
  E_1 FULL_HISTORY_RAW_COUNTS_V1: Historical Appearance Frequency - Ranks every number by how many times it was drawn in all earlier draws of the current era.
  E_2 RECUR_MEAN_ALL_V1: Expected Recurrence-Interval Alignment - Ranks every number by how close its next draw would come to completing its usual gap (the average of all its gaps).
  E_2 RECUR_MEAN_LAST8_V1: Expected Recurrence-Interval Alignment - Ranks every number by how close its next draw would come to completing its usual gap (the average of its last 8 gaps).
  E_2 RECUR_MEDIAN_ALL_V1: Expected Recurrence-Interval Alignment - Ranks every number by how close its next draw would come to completing its usual gap (the middle of all its gaps).
  E_3 POLY_INTERP_W52_D4_H4_V1: Occurrence-Curve Endpoint Forecast - Ranks every number by the fitted curve's value at the next draw, using a polynomial of degree 4 through 5 smoothed points of the last 52 draws fitted to its record of being drawn or not.
  E_3 POLY_LS_W104_D3_V1: Occurrence-Curve Endpoint Forecast - Ranks every number by the fitted curve's value at the next draw, using a least-squares polynomial of degree 3 over the last 104 draws fitted to its record of being drawn or not.
  E_3 POLY_LS_W52_D2_V1: Occurrence-Curve Endpoint Forecast - Ranks every number by the fitted curve's value at the next draw, using a least-squares polynomial of degree 2 over the last 52 draws fitted to its record of being drawn or not.
  E_3 POLY_LS_W52_D3_V1: Occurrence-Curve Endpoint Forecast - Ranks every number by the fitted curve's value at the next draw, using a least-squares polynomial of degree 3 over the last 52 draws fitted to its record of being drawn or not.
  E_3 POLY_MINIMAX_W52_D2_H4_V1: Occurrence-Curve Endpoint Forecast - Ranks every number by the fitted curve's value at the next draw, using a Chebyshev minimax polynomial of degree 2 over the last 52 draws, smoothed fitted to its record of being drawn or not.
  E_3 POLY_MINIMAX_W52_D3_H4_V1: Occurrence-Curve Endpoint Forecast - Ranks every number by the fitted curve's value at the next draw, using a Chebyshev minimax polynomial of degree 3 over the last 52 draws, smoothed fitted to its record of being drawn or not.
  E_4 POLY_INTERP_W52_D4_H4_V1: Occurrence-Curve Gradient Forecast - Ranks every number by the fitted curve's slope at the next draw, using a polynomial of degree 4 through 5 smoothed points of the last 52 draws fitted to its record of being drawn or not.
  E_4 POLY_LS_W104_D3_V1: Occurrence-Curve Gradient Forecast - Ranks every number by the fitted curve's slope at the next draw, using a least-squares polynomial of degree 3 over the last 104 draws fitted to its record of being drawn or not.
  E_4 POLY_LS_W52_D2_V1: Occurrence-Curve Gradient Forecast - Ranks every number by the fitted curve's slope at the next draw, using a least-squares polynomial of degree 2 over the last 52 draws fitted to its record of being drawn or not.
  E_4 POLY_LS_W52_D3_V1: Occurrence-Curve Gradient Forecast - Ranks every number by the fitted curve's slope at the next draw, using a least-squares polynomial of degree 3 over the last 52 draws fitted to its record of being drawn or not.
  E_4 POLY_MINIMAX_W52_D2_H4_V1: Occurrence-Curve Gradient Forecast - Ranks every number by the fitted curve's slope at the next draw, using a Chebyshev minimax polynomial of degree 2 over the last 52 draws, smoothed fitted to its record of being drawn or not.
  E_4 POLY_MINIMAX_W52_D3_H4_V1: Occurrence-Curve Gradient Forecast - Ranks every number by the fitted curve's slope at the next draw, using a Chebyshev minimax polynomial of degree 3 over the last 52 draws, smoothed fitted to its record of being drawn or not.
  E_6 BETA_BERNOULLI_DECAY_149_150_V1: Per-Number Fitted Distribution Models - Estimates each number's own chance of being drawn from earlier draws, each older draw counting 149/150 as much as the next (about two years' half-life), starting from the fair-draw chance.
  E_6 BETA_BERNOULLI_DECAY_74_75_V1: Per-Number Fitted Distribution Models - Estimates each number's own chance of being drawn from earlier draws, each older draw counting 74/75 as much as the next (about a year's half-life), starting from the fair-draw chance.
  E_6 BETA_BERNOULLI_FULL_MEMORY_V1: Per-Number Fitted Distribution Models - Estimates each number's own chance of being drawn from all earlier draws of the current era, counted equally, starting from the fair-draw chance.
  E_7 BETA_SHRINK_FULL_C52_V1: Bayesian Frequency Shrinkage - Ranks every number by the count over all earlier draws, pulled towards the fair rate by a prior worth 52 fair draws.
  E_7 BETA_SHRINK_W104_C52_V1: Bayesian Frequency Shrinkage - Ranks every number by the count over the last 104 draws (about two years), pulled towards the fair rate by a prior worth 52 fair draws.
  E_7 BETA_SHRINK_W52_C52_V1: Bayesian Frequency Shrinkage - Ranks every number by the count over the last 52 draws (about a year), pulled towards the fair rate by a prior worth 52 fair draws.
  E_8 HIER_PERIODS_P13_V1: Hierarchical Dirichlet-Multinomial Propensity - Ranks every number by a blend of each number's latest-period rate and its long-run rate, with periods of 13 draws (about a quarter), weighted by how much the periods differ beyond chance.
  E_8 HIER_PERIODS_P26_V1: Hierarchical Dirichlet-Multinomial Propensity - Ranks every number by a blend of each number's latest-period rate and its long-run rate, with periods of 26 draws (about half a year), weighted by how much the periods differ beyond chance.
  E_8 HIER_PERIODS_P52_V1: Hierarchical Dirichlet-Multinomial Propensity - Ranks every number by a blend of each number's latest-period rate and its long-run rate, with periods of 52 draws (about a year), weighted by how much the periods differ beyond chance.
  E_9 EXP_DECAY_18_19_V1: Exponentially Decayed Occurrence Rates - Ranks every number by its appearances, each older one counting for less, halving after about 13 draws (a quarter).
  E_9 EXP_DECAY_37_38_V1: Exponentially Decayed Occurrence Rates - Ranks every number by its appearances, each older one counting for less, halving after about 26 draws (half a year).
  E_9 EXP_DECAY_5_6_V1: Exponentially Decayed Occurrence Rates - Ranks every number by its appearances, each older one counting for less, halving after about 4 draws (a month).
  E_10 CONTRAST_S13_L104_V1: Multi-Horizon Frequency Contrasts - Ranks every number by its rate in the last 13 draws minus its rate in the 91 draws before it.
  E_10 CONTRAST_S26_L208_V1: Multi-Horizon Frequency Contrasts - Ranks every number by its rate in the last 26 draws minus its rate in the 182 draws before it.
  E_10 CONTRAST_S52_FULL_V1: Multi-Horizon Frequency Contrasts - Ranks every number by its rate in the last 52 draws minus its rate in all earlier draws.
  E_17 HAZARD_BINS_C100_V1: Discrete Recurrence Hazard Curves - Ranks every number by the chance, learned from all earlier draws, that a number absent for as long as it has been comes up next.
  E_17 HAZARD_BINS_W208_C100_V1: Discrete Recurrence Hazard Curves - Ranks every number by the chance, learned from the last 208 draws, that a number absent for as long as it has been comes up next.
  E_17 HAZARD_LOGAGE_SIGN_V1: Discrete Recurrence Hazard Curves - Ranks every number by how long it has been absent, in the direction learned from the data (Method A).
  E_18 MIX4_ALL_C10_V1: Mixtures of Recurrence Modes - Ranks every number by its chance of returning now under its own mix of four return patterns, fitted to all its gaps.
  E_18 MIX4_W104_C10_V1: Mixtures of Recurrence Modes - Ranks every number by its chance of returning now under its own mix of four return patterns, fitted to its gaps within the last 104 draws.
  E_18 MIX4_W208_C10_V1: Mixtures of Recurrence Modes - Ranks every number by its chance of returning now under its own mix of four return patterns, fitted to its gaps within the last 208 draws.
  E_19 PERIODIC_T2_V1: Periodic Recurrence-Hazard Peaks - Ranks every number by a repeating 2-draw cycle in its returns, on top of the general effect of absence.
  E_19 PERIODIC_T3_V1: Periodic Recurrence-Hazard Peaks - Ranks every number by a repeating 3-draw cycle in its returns, on top of the general effect of absence.
  E_19 PERIODIC_T4_V1: Periodic Recurrence-Hazard Peaks - Ranks every number by a repeating 4-draw cycle in its returns, on top of the general effect of absence.
